Let's plan algorithm:

1. Load parent height_sequence_1 if exists; clip 0..1000; normalize to sum=1? We can keep. Compute score. Set best.

2. Create candidate sequences from parametric families:
   - normalized ramp with positive slope and quadratic shapes; maybe exponentials.
   - parent interpolated to chosen lengths.
   - optimized small/medium sequences.

3. Main search phases:
   A. Low-dimensional continuous optimization to improve shape up to n=~512/1024 using direct softmax L-BFGS with LSE exact objective.
   B. Multi-resolution upscaling: from best shape, resize to larger n via interpolation, then refine with direct L-BFGS on normalized variables (or logit) using FFT and LSE, plus subgradient peak polishing.
   C. Diverse multistart: several initial parameterizations at each length to avoid local minima.
   D. Final evaluation and return best.

Need optimize efficiently. Since objective scale invariant and nonnegative, parameterize p_i = exp(y_i)/sum exp(y_i) for variables y real. Score = 2n max(q) where q = conv(p,p) (since sum p=1 exactly). No clipping needed. G_new values small <1. Output p scaled to sum 1 (or any). Evaluator will accept; if p has zeros no issue.

Gradient for softmax + LSE:
Let p_i = exp(y_i)/S_y, S_y=∑exp(y_i). S=∑p=1 exactly (within numerics). q=conv(p,p). Objective F = max_k q_k (score=2nF). Use LSE: Φ = LSE(q; β)/β maybe. Actually q sum=1; max approx = (1/β) log ∑ e^{β q}. Let L= LSE(q,β). Gradient wrt p: dL/dp_j = 2(∂q/∂p_j)⋅ w, where w_k = e^{β q_k}/∑ e^{β q_k}. ∂q_k/∂p_j = p_j? Wait q=conv(p,p). For fixed p but gradient wrt p: q_k = ∑_i p_i p_{k-i}. ∂q_k/∂p_j = p_{k-j}+p_{j-k} (with valid indices) = (T_k p)_j where T_k p is convolution of delta_j with p? In vector, gradient = 2 cross_conv? Let's compute f=e^{a(x^2-1)} (monotone increasing if a>0) for a moderate. Use asymptotic / numeric? Could choose a=5? Let's approximate with Laplace to balance peaks.

Define f(x)=exp(a (x^2-1)) / Z. For a large, f dominated near x=1. Let u=1-x. x^2-1=-2u+u^2. f≈exp(a u^2 -2a u)/Z, Z≈∫0^∞ e^{-2a u + a u^2} du ≈1/(2a) for a not too large. So f(x)≈2a e^{-2a u + a u^2}. Approx f≈2a e^{-2a u}. Sum distribution near right t=2-δ: y=1-u, z=1-v, u+v=δ. f(y)f(z)≈4a^2 e^{-2a(u+v)} times corrections =4a^2 e^{-2aδ}. Overlap length δ (u,v≥0). g≈4a^2 δ e^{-2aδ}, peak δ=1/(2a), height≈4a^2*(1/(2a))*e^{-1}=2a/e. Center t=1: y=x, z=1-x=u. g(1)=∫ f(y)f(1-y)dy. For large a, integrand for y away from1: both y and1-y? Let's outline top-level helper functions:

- `next_pow2(m)`: return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
- `fft_conv_full(a,b,nfft=None)`: linear convolution using rfft. If nfft None, next_pow2(len(a)+len(b)-1). Returns real array. Use np.fft.rfft. Could have negative small imaginary. Use `np.fft.irfft`. For nfft maybe pass.

- `softmax_p(y)`: stable.
- `softmax_score_grad(y, beta)`:
    p=softmax_p(y)
    n=len(p)
    nfft = next_pow2(2*n-1)
    q = self_convolve(p,nfft)

    F, grad = lse_from_p(p,q,beta,nfft,n)
    return F, grad
- `lse_from_p(p,q,beta,nfft)`:
    cmax=q.max(); z=beta*(q-cmax); z-=z.max(); exp_z=np.exp(z); s=exp_z.sum(); L = cmax + np.log(s)/beta
    w = exp_z/s
    # r = 2 * A where A_j = sum_i p_i w_{i+j}
    p_rev=p[::-1]
    PR=np.fft.rfft(p_rev,nfft)
    W=np.fft.rfft(w,nfft)
   新游? A_full=np.fft.irfft(PR*W,nfft)
    A = A_full[n-1:n]  # n length. Actually n-1 to 2n-2 inclusive -> slice [n-1: n-1+n]. Since A_full length nfft, slice [n-1:n-1+n].
    r=2*A
    C = np.dot(p,r)
    F = 2.0*n*(L) # or cmax? For beta finite L. For exact? Use L.
    grad = 2.0*n*p*(r-C)
    return F, grad

Let's design phases more concretely:

```
def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400):
    t0=time.monotonic(); deadline=t0+budget_s-30
    rng=default_rng(123)
    best_a=None; best_sc=inf
    parent = load_parent()
    if parent: report(...)
    # choose lengths: maybe [256,1024,4096,8192, parentlen]
    # But optimize small maybe skip if direct starts good.
```

We have to be mindful of total time. Let's allocate:
- 5% initial starts scoring (50 starts at final n) + evaluate parent.
- 70% main local optimization on top 3-5 starts at final length, with beta schedule and maybe variance.
- 15% medium length explore (1024/4096) and combine/interpolate to final, optimize briefly.
- 10% final polish on best.

But small/medium may find new basin not in final direct starts. We can include.

Main LBFGS optimization routine:
`polish_y(y, deadline, label, beta_list, use_var=False, mu=..., maxit_per_beta=2000)`:
- pre-center y
- for each beta in beta_list:
    set globals beta,mu
    while time<deadline - reserve and need more:
       y = centered
       res = minimize(lbfgs_obj, y, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True, options maxiter=chunk, maxfun=chunk*10, ftol=1e-14, gtol=1e-8)
       y=res.x
       evaluate exact score from p, report
       if res.status maybe converged; break to next beta.
    check time
- return y,p,sc
```

But if we run many chunks at same beta, after convergence no improvement; switch. We need pass y. Setting global beta; `lbfgs_obj` uses current. However scipy minimize may call objective after we change beta? We'll not change inside. Good.

Now, L-BFGS global functions:
```
_LBFGS_BETA = 100.0
_LBFGS_MU = 0.0
_LBFGS_NFFT = 0

def lbfgs_objective(x):
    p = softmax_p(x)
    n = len(p)
    nfft = next_pow2(2*n-1)
    q = self_convolve(p, nfft)
    qsum = q.sum()
    if qsum <=0: return ...
    q = q / qsum
    cmax = q.max()
    z = _LBFGS_BETA*(q-cmax)
    z = z - z.max()
    ez = np.exp(z)
    es = ez.sum()
    L = cmax + np.log(es)/_LBFGS_BETA
    F = 2.0*n*L
    w = ez/es
    A = cross_corr_sum(p, w, nfft)
    r = 2.0*A
    C = float(np.dot(p,r))
    grad = 2.0*n*p*(r-C)
    if _LBFGS_MU != 0.0:
        c = 1.0/(2*n-1)
        D = q - c
        W = n * float(np.dot(D,D))
        H = cross_corr_sum(p, D, nfft)
        grad = grad + _LBFGS_MU * (4.0*H)
        F = F + _LBFGS_MU * W
    # sanitize grad
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
    return F, grad
```

Check variance gradient scaling: Objective W=n*sumD^2. dW/dp = 2n∑D * ∂q/∂p. ∂q/∂p_j =2A_j(D?) where A_j=∑p_i D_{i+j}. So dW/dp =4n A. In grad for score F=2nL, Fgrad already 2n*p*(r-C). For W, grad_y = p*(∂W/∂p? formula p*(g - p·g) with g=dF/dp? Actually for softmax, if objective O(p), gradient wrt y is p*(g - C), C=∑p g. If we add W to p-objective, we should compute gradient as p*(g_total - C_total). If we simply add 4nA to grad from LSE, we miss softmax projection. Need careful.

We currently compute Fgrad = 2n p*(r-C_L) where C_L=∑p r. For additional W, if we just add raw 4nA to y-gradient, it's not projected along y; invariance under shifts would be broken. Better compute combined g_p = r + μ_w * (4? wait units) and then project once: grad_y = p*(g_p - C_total), F=2nL + μ n∑D^2? But Fgrad scale: r is derivative of L (not score). Score derivative =2n r. If objective O(p)=2n L + μ n∑D^2, then g_p = 2n r + μ n*4A. Then grad_y = p*(g_p - C), C=∑p g_p. F=O. This is correct. My previous separate added raw 4nA is wrong. Also `mu` should have same units. Let's define:
- L_p (not score) derivative r.
- Score S = 2n L.
- W = n ∑D^2 (scale O(1)). Its derivative w_p = 4n A. We'll add `var_coeff * w_p` to g_p.
So global `_VAR_COEF` default 0.
In objective:
```
r = 2*A_w
g_p = 2.0*n*r
if mu:
   g_p = g_p + mu * 4.0*n*A_D
C = dot(p,g_p)
grad = p*(g_p - C)
F = 2.0*n*L + mu*W
```
But `r` already includes derivative of L wrt p (with factor2). Good. `A_D` = cross_corr_sum(p,D). Then w_p = 2? Let's recompute V=∑D^2. dV/dp_j = 2∑D * 2A_j =4A. W=nV => dW/dp=4nA. yes.
If `_VAR_COEF`=mu (maybe 0.001?) add.

For pure, grad=p*(2n r - p·(2n r)). Good.

Need ensure L used in F: L = cmax + log_s/beta. If beta large, L approx max.

Now, multi-start local optimization function:
```
def polish_y(y, deadline, label, beta_schedule=(100,500,2000,10000,30000), var_schedule=(0,0,0,0,0), chunk_iters=300, reserve=5):
    y_best=None; p_best=None; sc_best=inf
    y = center_y(y.copy())
    for beta, var_mu in zip...:
       _LBFGS_BETA=beta; _VAR_COEF=var_mu
       # maybe more chunks while time and improvements
       while time < deadline-reserve:
          res=minimize(lbfgs_objective,y,...maxiter=chunk_iters,maxfun=chunk_iters*10,ftol=1e-14,gtol=1e-6)
          y=res.x
          p=softmax_p(y); sc=compute_exact(p)
          update best
          if res.status not in (0,1,2) and no improvement? break? 
          if sc plateau and beta high? break chunk to next beta
       # after beta, maybe carry y if improved
    return y_best, p_best, sc_best
```

But setting global `_VAR_COEF` inside loop: top-level variables. In Python, to assign in a top-level function, use `global _LBFGS_BETA, _VAR_COEF`. But `polish_y` will be called inside `propose_candidate`; need `global`.

However, `lbfgs_objective` uses global values; if we call `polish_y` sequentially, okay. If some scipy internals keep reference, no concurrency.

One issue: If `polish_y` uses `minimize` with same `lbfgs_objective`, and between chunks we set beta, but `minimize` might retain numerical line search points? It starts fresh.

Need choose chunk size. We can adapt to n: `chunk_iters = max(50, min(1000, 2000000 // n))`? For n=8192, ~244. Smaller 128. Use 200. For n=1024, 500.

`maxfun` maybe 4*maxiter.

Convergence: If L-BFGS reaches gtol, continue to next beta. We can limit number chunks per beta, e.g., 3-5. Or time. Use `max_chunks_per_beta = 3` for high n, 5 low. But if need many, time.

```
def make_de_basis(n):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   cols=[x, (x-0.5)**2, (2*x-1)**3]
   for k in range(1,6):
       cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x))
       cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*k*x))
   B=np.column_stack(cols)
   # center columns to avoid constant? softmax invariant, but DE needs search center 0. We can subtract column means.
   B = B - B.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
   return B

_DE_BASIS=None; _DE_BETA=1000.0
def de_objective(theta):
    p=softmax_p(_DE_BASIS @ theta)
    F,_,_,_,_ = p_objective_parts(p, _DE_BETA, 0.0)
    return F
```

Now, let's detail `polish_y`:
```
def polish_y(y, end_time, label, beta_schedule=None, var_schedule=None, chunk_len=200):
    y = np.asarray(y,float).copy()
    y = center_y(y)
    best_sc=np.inf; best_y=y.copy(); best_p=None
    n=len(y)
    # initial exact
    p=softmax_p(y); sc=compute_score(p); update
    if beta_schedule is None: beta_schedule=[100,500,2000,8000,30000]
    if var_schedule is None: var_schedule=[0.05,0.03,0.0,0.0,0.0]
    for idx,beta in enumerate(beta_schedule):
       if time > end_time-20: break
       mu = var_schedule[idx] if idx < len else 0.0
       _LBFGS_BETA=beta; _VAR_COEF=mu
       # run up to 3 chunks while time
       for chunk in range(3):

Let's detail functions.

`next_pow2(m)`:
```
def next_pow2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (int(m)-1).bit_length()
```

`convolve_full(a,b,nfft=None)`:
```
def convolve_full(a,b,nfft=None):
    n=len(a); m=len(b)
    out_len=n+m-1
    if nfft is None: nfft=next_pow2(out_len)
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,nfft); B=np.fft.rfft(b,nfft)
    out=np.fft.irfft(A*B,nfft)
    return out[:out_len]
```
Real input; if a,b lists. Use dtype float.

`self_convolve(a,nfft=None)`: if len<32 maybe `np.convolve`? For exact small maybe. But FFT okay. For n=1, nfft=1. Use `convolve_full(a,a,nfft)`.

`normalize_seq(a, target_sum=1.0)`:
```
def normalize_seq(a):
    arr=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(len(arr))/len(arr)
    return arr/s * target_sum? We'll return sum1 by default.
```
Could allow target_sum. Not needed. If len=0, [0.5].

`interpolate_seq(a,n)`:
```
def interpolate_seq(a,n):
   a=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   if len(a)==n: return a.copy()
   old=np.linspace(0,1,len(a)); new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   b=np.interp(new,old,a)
   return normalize_seq(b)
```

`seq_to_log(a)`:
```
def seq_to_log(a):
   p=normalize_seq(a)
   p=np.maximum(p,1e-15)
   y=np.log(p)
   return y - y.mean()
```

`compute_score(a)`:
```
def compute_score(a):
   arr=np.asarray(a,float).ravel()
   arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
   n=arr.size
   s=arr.sum()
   if n==0 or s < 1e-12: return np.inf
   nfft=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   q=self_convolve(arr,nfft)
   m=q.max()
   if m <0: m=0
   return float(2.0*n*m/(s*s))
```

`center_y(y)`:
```
def center_y(y):
   y=np.asarray(y,float)
   if y.size==0: return y
   y=y-np.mean(y)
   # if large, also center max? Mean enough. To avoid overflow in softmax, objective handles max. But for L-BFGS memory, keep moderate: if max-y min >200, subtract max? That changes p? subtract max is adding constant, okay.
   rngyx=np.max(y)-np.min(y)
   if rngyx > 50.0:
       y = y - np.max(y)  # all <=0? then exp okay; but p same if add constant; subtract max yields max 0. However if we then subtract mean in next, okay.
       y = y - np.mean(y)
   return y
```
Subtracting max then mean is just adding constant overall, p same. Good.

`softmax_p(y)`:
```
def softmax_p(y):
   y=np.asarray(y,float).ravel()
   y=np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=100.0, neginf=-100.0)
   if y.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
   y = y - np.max(y)
   e=np.exp(y)
   s=e.sum()
   if s <= 1e-300:
       return np.ones_like(y)/len(y)
   return e/s
```
If y max 100 after posinf? exp under? max 0 after subtract.

`cross_corr_sum(p,w,nfft)`:
```
def cross_corr_sum(p,w,nfft):
    n=len(p)
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    PR=np.fft.rfft(p_rev,nfft)
    WR=np.fft.rfft(w,nfft)
    out=np.fft.irfft(PR*WR,nfft)
    return out[n-1:n-1+n]
```
If w length not nfft, okay. We must ensure nfft enough: input w length ≤ nfft. For self_conv, same nfft. For variance D length q length 2n-1 ≤ nfft.

Potential negative small in A; okay.

`p_objective_parts(p,beta,var_mu)`:
```
def p_objective_parts(p,beta,var_mu=0.0):
   p=np.asarray(p,float).ravel()
   n=len(p)
   s=p.sum()
   if s <= 1e-12:
      p=np.ones(n)/n
   else: p=p/s
   nfft=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   q=convolve_full(p,p,nfft)
   qs=q.sum()
   if qs <= 1e-300:
      # uniform fallback
      q=np.ones(2*n-1)/(2*n-1)
   else:
      q=q/qs
   cmax=float(q.max())
   if beta <=0: beta=1e-6
   z=beta*(q-cmax)
   z-=z.max()
   ez=np.exp(z)
   es=ez.sum()
   if es <=0: uniform_ez
   else: ez/=es
   L=cmax + float(np.log(es))/beta
   F=2.0*n*L
   w=ez
   A=cross_corr_sum(p,w,nfft)
   r=2.0*A
   C=float(np.dot(p,r))
   g_p=2.0*n*(r-C)
   if var_mu != 0.0:
      c=1.0/(2.0*n-1.0)
      D=q-c
      W=float(n * np.dot(D,D))
      H=cross_corr_sum(p,D,nfft)
      g_p = g_p + var_mu * 4.0 * n * H
      F = F + var_mu * W
   return F, g_p, r, C, q
```
Here `w` normalized (ez sum1). Good. Note `A` from cross_corr_sum with w; r derivative of L? L=LSE(q)/beta? Wait L defined cmax + log(es)/beta, where w=exp(beta(q-cmax))/es = derivative of L wrt q. ∂L/∂q_k = w_k. Then ∂L/∂p =2A. Yes. F=2nL. Good.

If var_mu added to F, g_p derivative of F wrt p (not y). Good.

`lbfgs_objective(x)`:
```
def lbfgs_objective(x):
    p=softmax_p(x)
    mu=_VAR_COEF
    F,g_p,r,C,q = p_objective_parts(p,_LBFGS_BETA,mu)
    g_p = np.nan_to_num(g_p,0,1e6,-1e6)
    Cg=float(np.dot(p,g_p))
    grad=p*(g_p-Cg)
    grad=np.nan_to_num(grad,0,1e6,-1e6)
    F=float(np.nan_to_num(F, nan=1e8, posinf=1e8, neginf=1e8))
    return F, grad
```
This uses global `_LBFGS_BETA`, `_VAR_COEF`. Need declare globals? Not assigning, no.

```
def generate_y_starts(n, rng, parent=None, max_starts=40):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   starts=[]
   add = lambda? no lambda, use helper top-level? We can just append.
   if parent is not None:
      b=interpolate_seq(parent,n); starts.append(seq_to_log(b))
   starts.append(np.zeros(n))
   # linear
   for a in [-2.0,-1.0,-0.5,0.5,1.0,2.0]:
      f=0.05 + 1.0 + a*(2*x-1)
      if f.min()<=0: f=np.maximum(f,1e-4)
      starts.append(seq_to_log(f))
   # quad
   for a in [-0.8,-0.4,0.0,0.4,0.8]:
      for b in [-0.8,-0.4,0.0,0.4,0.8]:
         f=0.01 + 1.0 + a*(2*x-1) + b*(2*x-1)**2
         if f.min() <= 0: f=np.maximum(f,1e-4)
         starts.append(seq_to_log(f))
   # exp quadratic log densities directly
   for a in [-3.0,-1.5,0.0,1.5,3.0]:
      for b in [-3.0,-1.0,0.0,1.0,3.0]:
         y=a*x + b*(x-0.5)**2
         starts.append(center_y(y))
   # powers
   eps=1e-6
   for aa in [0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0]:
      for bb in [0.2,0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0]:
         f=np.clip(x,1e-4,1-1e-4)**aa * np.clip(1-x,1e-4,1-1e-4)**bb
         if f.sum()<=0: continue
         starts.append(seq_to_log(f))
   # cos
   for mamp in [(1,0.4,0.0),(2,0.4,0.0),(3,0.3,0.0),(1,0.5,0.3),(2,0.3,0.4)]:
       m,a1,a2=mamp
       f=0.7 + a1*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x) + a2*np.cos(4*np.pi*m*x)
       if f.min()<=0: f=np.maximum(f,1e-4)
       starts.append(seq_to_log(f))
   # random smooth
   for _ in range(12):
       y=np.zeros(n)
       for k in range(1,8):
           amp=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.4))
           ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
           y += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x + ph)
       if rng.random()<0.5:
           y += float(rng.uniform(-1,1))*x
       starts.append(center_y(y))
   # cap max
   starts = [center_y(s) for s in starts if len(s)==n]
   return starts[:max_starts]
```

def multiplicative_polish(p, end_time, label, rng=None):
   if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(1)
   p=normalize_seq(p); n=len(p); best...
   no=0; beta=5000
   while time < end_time - 30 and no < 300:
      F,g_p,r,C,q = p_objective_parts(p,beta,0.0)
      sc_cur = 2*n*float(q.max())
      update best
      d=(r-C)*n
      maxd=float(np.max(np.abs(d))) if d.size else 1.0
      if maxd <1e-12: break? perturb
      d_use = d/max(maxd,1e-12)
      improved=False
      for step in [0.4,0.2,0.1,0.05,0.02]:
          ratio=np.exp(-step*d_use)
          p_try=p*ratio

             amp=0.1 + 0.2*min(1.0,no/100)
             for k in range(1,10): ... y += amp*rng... 
             p=softmax_p(center_y(y))
             # also maybe exact update
   return best...

Detailed `polish_y` implementation:
```
def polish_y(y, end_time, label, beta_schedule=None, var_schedule=None, chunk_len=None):
    global _LBFGS_BETA, _VAR_COEF
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if y.size == 0: return None, None, np.inf
    if not hasattr(end_time, '__float__'): end_time=float(end_time)
    y = center_y(y)
    best_y = y.copy()
    best_p = softmax_p(best_y)
    best_sc = compute_score(best_p)
    if beta_schedule is None:
        beta_schedule = [50.0, 200.0, 1000.0, 4000.0, 12000.0, 30000.0]
    if var_schedule is None:
        var_schedule = [0.05, 0.03, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0]
    n = y.size
    if chunk_len is None:
        chunk_len = int(max(80, min(600, 1200000 // max(1, n))))
    for bi, beta in enumerate(beta_schedule):
        if time.monotonic() > end_time - 15.0: break
        mu = var_schedule[bi] if bi < len(var_schedule) else 0.0
        _LBFGS_BETA = float(beta)
        _VAR_COEF = float(mu)
        # up to 4 chunks
        for chunk in range(4):
            if time.monotonic() > end_time - 10.0: break
            # compute current obj to compare
            try:
                obj_before, grad_before = lbfgs_objective(y)
            except Exception:
                break
            if not np.isfinite(obj_before): break
            try:
                res = minimize(lbfgs_objective, y, method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                               options={'maxiter': chunk_len, 'maxfun': 4*chunk_len,
                                        'ftol':1e-14, 'gtol':1e-7, 'maxls':50})
            except Exception:
                break
            y = center_y(np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel())
            p = softmax_p(y)
            sc = compute_score(p)
            if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_sc = sc; best_y=y.copy(); best_p=p.copy(); print(label + ' exact ' + repr(sc))
            # smooth improvement check
            if not np.isfinite(res.fun) or res.fun > obj_before + 1e-12:
                break
        # after beta, if best p changed maybe continue
    # Carry best_y but if best_sc came from previous beta, maybe use best_y for return; y may be last. We'll return best_y.
    if best_p is None:
        best_p = softmax_p(best_y)
    return best_y, best_p, best_sc
```
The smooth improvement check `res.fun > obj_before +1e-12` breaks immediately if no smooth progress. But if `lbfgs_objective` has noise, could break. Use tolerance 1e-10. Also if exact improved but smooth not, maybe still continue? We can break to next beta if smooth no improvement. Good.

Potential infinite if y not changed. `res.status` maybe 0 success; we check fun. If success but no fun decrease due tolerance, break.

What if global mu changes, `obj_before` from previous global? We set global before, okay.

When `res.fun` may be `inf`; break.

Printing inside helper okay. Add label.

`multiplicative_polish` implementation with exact update:
```
def multiplicative_polish(p, end_time, label, rng=None):
    if rng is None: rng=np.random.default_rng(7)
    p=normalize_seq(p)
    n=p.size
    best_p=p.copy(); best_sc=compute_score(p)
    no=0; beta=5000.0
    while time.monotonic() < end_time - 15.0 and no < 250:
       try:
          F,g_p,r,C,q = p_objective_parts(p,beta,0.0)
       except Exception:
          break
       cur_sc=2.0*n*float(q.max())  # since p sum1; but q normalized, exact maybe compute_score
       if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-14: best update
       d=(r-C)*n
       maxd=float(np.max(np.abs(d))) if d.size else 0.0
       if not np.isfinite(maxd) or maxd < 1e-14:
          maxd=1.0
       d_use = d / maxd
       improved=False
       for step in (0.35,0.18,0.09,0.045,0.02):
           ratio = np.exp(-step*d_use)
           p_try = p * ratio
           s=p_try.sum()
           if s <= 1e-300: continue
           p_try = p_try / s
           sc = compute_score(p_try)
           if sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
               best_sc=sc; best_p=p_try.copy(); p=p_try; improved=True; break
       if improved:
          no=0
          beta = min(beta*1.4, 150000.0)
          if best_sc < 1.6: maybe
       else:
          no += 1
          if no % 25 == 0:
             y=np.log(np.maximum(p,1e-15))
             y=y-y.mean()
             amp=0.05 + 0.1*min(1.0, no/80.0)
             nuse=y.size
             x=np.linspace(0,1,nuse)
             for k in range(1,12):
                ampk=amp/(k**0.7)
                ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
                y = y + ampk*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x + ph)
                y = y + ampk*np.sin(2*np.pi*k*x + ph)
             p=softmax_p(center_y(y))
             sc=compute_score(p)
             if sc < best_sc -1e-14: best update
    if best_sc > compute_score(best_p): ...
    return best_p, best_sc
```
Here `q` normalized, p sum1, exact score =2n max(q)/1. q may have negative tiny; max. Good.

`compute_score(p_try)` recomputes q. Could optimize by using self_conv directly but fine.

Number iterations: Each loop 1 p_objective + up to 5 compute_score. If no improve fast, maybe 250*6*0.005=7.5s for n=8192; okay. If n=12288 maybe 15s. Good. Could run longer.

Potential bug: In multiplicative, if no improvement because step zero due d_use? Perturb after 25. Good.

Now, `load_parent` top-level:
```
def load_parent():
    obj = globals().get('height_sequence_1', None)
    if obj is None: return None
    try:
       arr=np.asarray(obj,dtype=np.float64).ravel()
       if arr.size < 16: return None
       arr=np.clip(arr,0,1000)
       if np.any(np.isnan(arr)): arr=np.nan_to_num(arr,0)
       s=arr.sum()
       if s <=0: return None
       return arr
    except Exception:
       return None
```

Now, think about math correctness of `p_objective_parts` with `var_mu`: We compute `H=cross_corr_sum(p,D,nfft)`. If D sum=0, gradient g_p should be orthogonal? g_p_raw = 2n(r-C) + mu*4nH. Sum over p of g_p = 0 by construction. Good. The objective F includes var but our `lbfgs_objective` projects g_p again: `Cg=dot(p,g_p); grad=p*(g_p-Cg)`. But g_p is already derivative of O(p) wrt p (not projected). Is it okay to project after? Yes: gradient wrt y = p*(g_p - p·g_p). Even if g_p includes score and variance. Good.

For `multiplicative_polish`, we use pure, no var. Good.

Let's verify `cross_corr_sum` derivative orientation with n=3 example to ensure. p=[a,b,c]. w length5. r_j? derivative of L wrt p_j = 2 ∑_{i=0}^{2} p_i w_{i+j}. For j=0: 2(p0 w0 + p1 w1 + p2 w2). Cross: p_rev=[c,b,a], conv with w, coeff m=2: c*w2 + b*w3 + a*w4? That's not p0 w0 + p1 w1 + p2 w2. Did we index wrong! Let's re-evaluate! Important.

We need A_j = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1} p_i w_{i+j} for j=0..n-1. This is cross-correlation of p with w with positive lag j (w index i+j). How compute via FFT? We proposed p_rev*w coeff n-1+j. Let's test n=3 (N maybe 5). p_rev = [c,b,a]. Conv c = p_rev*w: c_m=∑_{i=0}^{2} p_rev_i w_{m-i}. We want m=n-1+j=2+j. For j=0, m=2: c*b? terms i=0: c*w2; i=1:b*w1; i=2:a*w0. Sum = c w2 + b w1 + a w0 = p2 w2 + p1 w1 + p0 w0. That's A_0 = p0 w0 + p1 w1 + p2 w2 (same order). Great. I mistakenly used w4. Good. For j=1 m=3: c w3 + b w2 + a w1 = p2 w3? w length5, p2 w_{? i=2,j=1=>w3}, p1 w2, p0 w1. Yes A1. For j=2 m=4: c w4 + b w3 + a w2 = p2 w4,p1 w3,p0 w2. Good. So slice [n-1:n-1+n] works.

N needs ≥ max m + max index? We only need m up to 2n-2. But full conv length of p_rev (n) and w (2n-1) is 3n-2. With N=next_pow2(2n-1), circular wrap could contaminate coefficient m if m+N within full length. For n=3, N=8? 2n-1=5 next8. No wrap. For n=4, N=8? 2n-1=7 next8. Full conv length 11 (0..10). m max 6. m+N max14 >10, no wrap. But what about coefficients m with i>m? m min n-1=3. p_rev support i=0..3. For m=3, i=3 only i=m; no i>m. So no wrap. For general N=2n-1 (or next pow2 larger maybe >2n-1). If N>2n-1, full conv length 3n-2, m+N may be ≤ full length? For m=n-1, N maybe power2 up to 4n? If N=16384 for n=8192? actually N=16384, 2n-1=16383, full conv len 24575 (0..24574), m+N =8191+16384=24575 > max; no wrap. For m max 2n-2=16382, m+N=32766 > max. Good. If N much larger than 2n-1 (we choose next_pow2(2n-1), so N<2*(2n-1). m+N max (2n-2)+N <4n, which could exceed full max 3n-3 if N< n+? For next power, N≥2n-1, m+N min for m=n-1: n-1+N >3n-2? If N=2n-1, min=3n-2, full max=3n-3, no wrap. If N larger, also no. Great.

Now, self_conv nfft same N. q length 2n-1. Good.

```python
"""
Hybrid multi-start optimizer for
    minimize 2 n max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)^2
over non-negative sequences.

Strategy:
- Work on the probability simplex p = a / sum(a).
- Optimize p through y = log(p), i.e. p = softmax(y).
- Use an FFT-based LSE-smoothed maximum-convolution objective with accurate
  FFT gradients.  The objective can optionally include a variance term to
  encourage a flatter convolution during exploration.
- Run L-BFGS in chunked time-limited calls for several beta values.
- Generate diverse smooth starts, including a small differential-evolution
  search over a reduced smooth basis.
- Finish with a multiplicative peak-reduction polish.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize, differential_evolution

_LBFGS_BETA = 1000.0
_VAR_COEF = 0.0
_DE_BASIS = None
_DE_BETA = 2000.0


def next_pow2(m):
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def convolve_full(a, b, nfft=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    out_len = len(a) + len(b) - 1
    if out_len <= 0:
        return np.array([])
    if nfft is None:
        nfft = next_pow2(out_len)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, nfft)
    fb = np.fft.rfft(b, nfft)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, nfft)
    return out[:out_len]


def self_convolve(a, nfft=None):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size <= 0:
        return np.array([])
    return convolve_full(a, a, nfft)


def normalize_seq(a, target_sum=1.0):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.array([0.5], dtype=np.float64) * target_sum
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.full(arr.shape, target_sum / arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return arr * (target_sum / s)


def interpolate_seq(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
    if a.size == n:
        return a.copy()
    if a.size == 1:
        return np.full(n, float(a[0]), dtype=np.float64)
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size, dtype=np.float64)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    b = np.interp(new, old, a)
    return normalize_seq(b)


def seq_to_log(a):
    p = normalize_seq(a)
    p = np.maximum(p, 1e-15)
    y = np.log(p)
    return y - float(np.mean(y))


def compute_score(a):
    arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    n = arr.size
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    nfft = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    q = self_convolve(arr, nfft)
    if q.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = float(np.max(q))
    if m < 0.0:
        m = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def center_y(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if y.size == 0:
        return y
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=100.0, neginf=-100.0)
    y -= float(np.mean(y))
    spread = float(np.max(y) - np.min(y))
    if spread > 50.0:
        y -= float(np.max(y))
        y -= float(np.mean(y))
    return y


def softmax_p(y):
    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if y.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=100.0, neginf=-100.0)
    y = y - float(np.max(y))
    e = np.exp(y)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(y.shape, 1.0 / y.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def cross_corr_sum(p, w, nfft):
    """
    A_j = sum_i p_i w_{i+j}, for j=0..n-1.
    This is a one-sided cross-correlation needed for self-convolution gradients.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = p.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    p_rev = p[::-1]
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p_rev, nfft)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, nfft)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fp * fw, nfft)
    return out[n - 1:n - 1 + n]


def p_objective_parts(p, beta, var_mu=0.0):
    """
    Return (F, grad_p, r, C, q_normalized) for p normalized to sum 1.
    grad_p is the gradient with respect to p (not yet projected to log-density y).
    r is used by multiplicative polish as d = r - C for the LSE part.
    """
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.inf, np.array([], dtype=np.float64), np.array([], dtype=np.float64), 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        p = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        p = p / s

    nfft = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    q = self_convolve(p, nfft)
    if q.size == 0:
        return np.inf, np.zeros(n), np.zeros(n), 0.0, np.array([], dtype=np.float64)

    qs = float(q.sum())
    if qs <= 1e-300:
        q = np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64) / float(2 * n - 1)
    else:
        q = q / qs

    cmax = float(np.max(q))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax):
        cmax = 0.0

    if beta <= 1e-6:
        beta = 1e-6
    z = float(beta) * (q - cmax)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    ez = np.exp(z)
    es = float(ez.sum())
    if es <= 1e-300:
        ez = np.ones(q.shape, dtype=np.float64) / q.size
    else:
        ez = ez / es

    L = cmax + float(np.log(es)) / float(beta)
    F = 2.0 * n * L
    w = ez

    A = cross_corr_sum(p, w, nfft)
    r = 2.0 * A
    C = float(np.dot(p, r))
    g_p = 2.0 * n * (r - C)

    if var_mu != 0.0:
        c_flat = 1.0 / float(2 * n - 1)
        D = q - c_flat
        W = n * float(np.dot(D, D))
        H = cross_corr_sum(p, D, nfft)
        g_p = g_p + float(var_mu) * 4.0 * n * H
        F = F + float(var_mu) * W

    F = float(np.nan_to_num(F, nan=1e8, posinf=1e8, neginf=1e8))
    g_p = np.nan_to_num(g_p, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
    return F, g_p, r, C, q


def lbfgs_objective(x):
    p = softmax_p(x)
    F, g_p, r, C, q = p_objective_parts(p, _LBFGS_BETA, _VAR_COEF)
    Cg = float(np.dot(p, g_p))
    grad = p * (g_p - Cg)
    grad = np.nan_to_num(grad, nan=0.0, posinf=1e6, neginf=-1e6)
    return float(F), grad


def polish_y(y, end_time, label, beta_schedule=None, var_schedule=None, chunk_len=None):
    global _LBFGS_BETA, _VAR_COEF

    y = np.asarray(y, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if y.size == 0:
        return None, None, np.inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(y)):
        y = np.nan_to_num(y, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    y = center_y(y)

    best_y = y.copy()
    best_p = softmax_p(best_y)
    best_sc = compute_score(best_p)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best_sc = 10.0

    if beta_schedule is None:
        beta_schedule = [100.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 8000.0, 30000.0]
    if var_schedule is None:
        var_schedule = [0.05, 0.02, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0]

    n = y.size
    if chunk_len is None:
        chunk_len = int(max(80, min(600, 1200000 // max(1, n))))

    for bi, beta in enumerate(beta_schedule):
        if time.monotonic() > float(end_time) - 15.0:
            break
        mu = float(var_schedule[bi]) if bi < len(var_schedule) else 0.0
        _LBFGS_BETA = float(beta)
        _VAR_COEF = mu

        for _chunk in range(4):
            if time.monotonic() > float(end_time) - 10.0:
                break
            try:
                obj_before, _ = lbfgs_objective(y)
            except Exception:
                break
            if not np.isfinite(obj_before):
                break

            options = {
                "maxiter": chunk_len,
                "maxfun": 4 * chunk_len,
                "ftol": 1e-14,
                "gtol": 1e-7,
                "maxls": 50,
                "maxcor": 50,
            }
            try:
                res = minimize(lbfgs_objective, y, method="L-BFGS-B", jac=True, options=options)
            except Exception:
                break

            y_new = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if y_new.size != n:
                break
            y_new = center_y(y_new)
            p_new = softmax_p(y_new)
            sc_new = compute_score(p_new)

            if np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_sc = float(sc_new)
                best_y = y_new.copy()
                best_p = p_new.copy()
                print(f"  [{label}] improved exact score to {best_sc:.10f}")

            y = y_new
            if not np.isfinite(getattr(res, "fun", np.inf)) or float(res.fun) > obj_before + 1e-10:
                break

    if best_p is None:
        best_p = softmax_p(best_y)
    sc_final = compute_score(best_p)
    if np.isfinite(sc_final) and sc_final < best_sc:
        best_sc = float(sc_final)
        best_p = softmax_p(best_y)
    return best_y, best_p, float(best_sc)


def multiplicative_polish(p, end_time, label, rng=None):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(12345)
    p = normalize_seq(p)
    if p.size == 0:
        return np.array([0.5]), np.inf

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_sc = compute_score(best_p)
    no_improve = 0
    beta = 5000.0

    while time.monotonic() < float(end_time) - 20.0 and no_improve < 350:
        try:
            F, g_p, r, C, q = p_objective_parts(p, beta, 0.0)
        except Exception:
            break

        if q.size > 0:
            cur_sc = 2.0 * p.size * float(np.max(q))
            if np.isfinite(cur_sc) and cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_sc = float(cur_sc)
                best_p = p.copy()

        d = (r - C) * float(p.size)
        maxd = float(np.max(np.abs(d))) if d.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(maxd) or maxd <= 1e-14:
            maxd = 1.0
        d_use = d / maxd

        improved = False
        for step in (0.25, 0.12, 0.06, 0.03):
            ratio = np.exp(-step * d_use)
            p_try = p * ratio
            s = float(p_try.sum())
            if s <= 1e-300:
                continue
            p_try = p_try / s
            sc_try = compute_score(p_try)
            if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try < best_sc - 1e-14:
                best_sc = float(sc_try)
                best_p = p_try.copy()
                p = p_try
                improved = True
                no_improve = 0
                print(f"  [{label}] multiplicative improved score to {best_sc:.10f}")
                break

        if improved:
            beta = min(beta * 1.4, 200000.0)
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve % 25 == 0:
                y = np.log(np.maximum(p, 1e-15))
                y = center_y(y)
                amp = 0.05 + 0.08 * min(1.0, float(no_improve) / 80.0)
                m = y.size
                x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m, dtype=np.float64)
                for k in range(1, 12):
                    ak = amp / (float(k) ** 0.7)
                    ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                    ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
                    y = y + ak * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph1)
                    y = y + ak * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph2)
                p = softmax_p(center_y(y))
                sc = compute_score(p)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-14:
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    best_p = p.copy()

    return best_p, float(best_sc)


def make_de_basis(n):
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    cols = []
    cols.append(x)
    cols.append((x - 0.5) ** 2)
    cols.append((2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 3)
    for k in range(1, 6):
        cols.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x))
        cols.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * x))
    B = np.column_stack(cols).astype(np.float64, copy=False)
    B -= B.mean(axis=0, keepdims=True)
    B = np.nan_to_num(B, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    return B


def de_objective(theta):
    if _DE_BASIS is None:
        return 1e8
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if theta.size != _DE_BASIS.shape[1]:
        return 1e8
    theta = np.nan_to_num(theta, nan=0.0, posinf=5.0, neginf=-5.0)
    p = softmax_p(_DE_BASIS @ theta)
    F, _, _, _, _ = p_objective_parts(p, _DE_BETA, 0.0)
    return float(F)


def generate_y_starts(n, rng, parent=None, max_starts=48, include_parent=True):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
    starts = []

    if include_parent and parent is not None:
        ppar = interpolate_seq(parent, n)
        starts.append(center_y(seq_to_log(ppar)))

    # Uniform.
    starts.append(np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64))

    # Linear antisymmetric shapes.
    for a in (-2.0, -1.2, -0.7, -0.35, 0.35, 0.7, 1.2, 2.0):
        f = 0.05 + 1.0 + a * (2.0 * x - 1.0)
        starts.append(center_y(seq_to_log(f)))

    # Bilinear / quadratic low-order polynomials.
    for a in (-0.8, -0.4, 0.0, 0.4, 0.8):
        for b in (-0.8, -0.4, 0.0, 0.4, 0.8):
            f = 0.01 + 1.0 + a * (2.0 * x - 1.0) + b * (2.0 * x - 1.0) ** 2
            starts.append(center_y(seq_to_log(f)))

    # Log-polynomial / log-quadratic shapes.
    for a in (-3.0, -1.8, -0.8, 0.0, 0.8, 1.8, 3.0):
        for b in (-3.0, -1.5, 0.0, 1.5, 3.0):
            y = a * x + b * (x - 0.5) ** 2
            starts.append(center_y(y))

    # Beta-like endpoint powers, allowing asymmetric endpoint behavior.
    epsx = 1e-5
    xc = np.clip(x, epsx, 1.0 - epsx)
    for aa in (0.15, 0.35, 0.7, 1.4, 2.5, 4.0):
        for bb in (0.15, 0.35, 0.7, 1.4, 2.5, 4.0):
            f = xc ** aa * (1.0 - xc) ** bb
            starts.append(center_y(seq_to_log(f)))

    # Low-frequency nonnegative waves.
    for m, a1, a2 in (
        (1, 0.35, 0.0),
        (2, 0.35, 0.0),
        (3, 0.25, 0.0),
        (1, 0.45, 0.25),
        (2, 0.25, 0.45),
        (4, 0.20, 0.10),
    ):
        f = 0.75 + a1 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x) + a2 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * 2 * m * x)
        starts.append(center_y(seq_to_log(f)))

    # Random smooth log-densities.
    for _ in range(14):
        y = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(1, 8):
            ampk = float(rng.uniform(0.03, 0.32)) / (float(k) ** 0.55)
            ph1 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            ph2 = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            y = y + ampk * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph1)
            y = y + ampk * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph2)
        if rng.random() < 0.65:
            y = y + float(rng.uniform(-1.2, 1.2)) * x
        starts.append(center_y(y))

    valid = []
    for s in starts:
        s = np.asarray(s, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if s.size == n and np.all(np.isfinite(s)):
            valid.append(center_y(s))

    if len(valid) > max_starts:
        idx = rng.permutation(len(valid))[:max_starts]
        valid = [valid[int(i)] for i in idx]
    return valid


def load_parent():
    obj = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    if obj is None:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(obj, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size < 16:
            return None
        arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
        arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if float(arr.sum()) <= 1e-12:
            return None
        return arr
    except Exception:
        return None


def propose_candidate(budget_s=2400.0):
    global _DE_BASIS, _DE_BETA, _LBFGS_BETA, _VAR_COEF

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    total = max(60.0, float(budget_s) - 45.0)
    deadline = t0 + total
    rng = np.random.default_rng(20260425)

    best_sc = np.inf
    best_a = np.full(1024, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)

    parent = load_parent()
    if parent is not None:
        pn = normalize_seq(parent)
        sc = compute_score(pn)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc)
            best_a = pn.copy()
        print(f"Parent loaded, len={parent.size}, score={best_sc:.10f}")

    n_main = 8192

    # Candidate starts at the main length.
    starts = []
    if parent is not None:
        py_par = seq_to_log(interpolate_seq(parent, n_main))
        starts.append(py_par)

    starts.extend(generate_y_starts(n_main, rng, parent=None, max_starts=56, include_parent=False))

    # Small reduced-basis global search to add one more diverse shape.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 220.0:
        try:
            B = make_de_basis(128)
            _DE_BASIS = B
            _DE_BETA = 2000.0
            dim = B.shape[1]
            bounds = [(-3.0, 3.0)] * dim
            try:
                de_res = differential_evolution(
                    de_objective,
                    bounds,
                    maxiter=25,
                    popsize=8,
                    seed=42,
                    tol=1e-4,
                    polish=False,
                    update_interval=0,
                )
            except TypeError:
                de_res = differential_evolution(
                    de_objective,
                    bounds,
                    maxiter=25,
                    popsize=8,
                    seed=42,
                    tol=1e-4,
                    polish=False,
                )
            if hasattr(de_res, "x") and len(de_res.x) == dim:
                p_de = softmax_p(B @ np.asarray(de_res.x, dtype=np.float64))
                p_de = interpolate_seq(p_de, n_main)
                sc = compute_score(p_de)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                    best_sc = float(sc)
                    best_a = p_de.copy()
                    print(f"DE shape improved score to {best_sc:.10f}")
                starts.append(center_y(seq_to_log(p_de)))
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Score all candidate starts exactly, then keep the strong few.
    scored = []
    for i, y in enumerate(starts):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline - 120.0:
            break
        try:
            p = softmax_p(y)
            sc = compute_score(p)
        except Exception:
            sc = np.inf
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), i, y))

    if len(scored) == 0:
        scored.append((compute_score(np.ones(n_main) / n_main), -1, np.zeros(n_main, dtype=np.float64)))

    scored.sort()
    top = []
    for tuple_item in scored[:6]:
        top.append(tuple_item[2])

    # Make sure the parent start is considered if present.
    top_final = []
    if parent is not None and len(starts) > 0:
        top_final.append(starts[0])
    for y in top:
        dup = False
        for t in top_final:
            if t.shape == y.shape and np.array_equal(t, y):
                dup = True
                break
        if not dup:
            top_final.append(y)
    top = top_final[:6]
    if len(top) == 0:
        top = [np.zeros(n_main, dtype=np.float64)]

    # Main multi-start L-BFGS phase.
    main_deadline = min(t0 + 0.68 * total, deadline)
    if time.monotonic() < main_deadline - 80.0:
        n_starts = max(1, len(top))
        chunk = max(45.0, (main_deadline - time.monotonic()) / float(n_starts) - 15.0)
        main_betas = [100.0, 500.0, 2000.0, 8000.0, 30000.0]
        main_vars = [0.05, 0.02, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0]

        for idx, y in enumerate(top):
            if time.monotonic() > main_deadline - 40.0:
                break
            end = time.monotonic() + chunk
            if end > main_deadline - 25.0:
                end = main_deadline - 25.0
            print(f"Main start {idx + 1}/{len(top)} starting, initial score={compute_score(softmax_p(y)):.10f}")
            ly, lp, ls = polish_y(
                y,
                end,
                f"main{idx}",
                beta_schedule=main_betas,
                var_schedule=main_vars,
                chunk_len=None,
            )
            if lp is not None and np.isfinite(ls) and ls < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(ls)
                best_a = lp.copy()
                print(f"Main start {idx + 1} best exact score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # Alternate length if there is meaningful time left.
    alt_deadline = min(t0 + 0.87 * total, deadline)
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < alt_deadline - 60.0:
        if parent is not None:
            plen = parent.size
            if 2048 <= plen <= 12288 and plen != best_a.size:
                n_alt = int(plen)
            else:
                n_alt = 10240
        else:
            n_alt = 10240

        if n_alt != best_a.size:
            y_alt = seq_to_log(interpolate_seq(best_a, n_alt))
            end = time.monotonic() + max(35.0, 0.10 * total)
            if end > alt_deadline - 25.0:
                end = alt_deadline - 25.0
            print(f"Alternate length {n_alt}")
            ly, lp, ls = polish_y(
                y_alt,
                end,
                "alt",
                beta_schedule=[500.0, 2000.0, 8000.0, 30000.0],
                var_schedule=[0.02, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0],
                chunk_len=None,
            )
            if lp is not None and np.isfinite(ls) and ls < best_sc:
                best_sc = float(ls)
                best_a = lp.copy()
                print(f"Alternate length best exact score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # Final high-beta L-BFGS refinement on the current best.
    final_lbfgs_deadline = min(t0 + 0.955 * total, deadline)
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < final_lbfgs_deadline - 45.0:
        y_best = seq_to_log(best_a)
        end = time.monotonic() + max(25.0, 0.035 * total)
        if end > final_lbfgs_deadline - 15.0:
            end = final_lbfgs_deadline - 15.0
        chunk = int(max(70, min(350, 700000 // max(1, best_a.size))))
        ly, lp, ls = polish_y(
            y_best,
            end,
            "finLB",
            beta_schedule=[5000.0, 20000.0, 80000.0],
            var_schedule=[0.0, 0.0, 0.0],
            chunk_len=chunk,
        )
        if lp is not None and np.isfinite(ls) and ls < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(ls)
            best_a = lp.copy()
            print(f"Final LBFGS best exact score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # Multiplicative peak-reduction polish using remaining time.
    if best_a is not None and time.monotonic() < deadline - 70.0:
        p_best = normalize_seq(best_a)
        p_best, sc_best = multiplicative_polish(p_best, deadline - 30.0, "multiplicative", rng)
        if np.isfinite(sc_best) and sc_best < best_sc:
            best_sc = float(sc_best)
            best_a = p_best.copy()
            print(f"Multiplicative polish best exact score={best_sc:.10f}")

    # Sanitize final output and verify with the official evaluator.
    out = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    out = np.nan_to_num(out, nan=0.0, posinf=1000.0, neginf=0.0)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-12:
        out = np.full(64, 0.5, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(out.sum())
    out = out / s

    try:
        verified = float(evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in out]))
    except Exception:
        verified = float(best_sc)

    print("=" * 70)
    print(f"FINAL internal exact score = {best_sc:.10f}")
    print(f"FINAL verified score       = {verified:.10f}")
    print(f"Length                     = {out.size}")
    print(f"Runtime                    = {time.monotonic() - t0:.1f}s / {float(budget_s):.1f}s")
    print("=" * 70)

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
